EVI 3 is the latest iteration, capable of streaming user speech and forming natural, expressive responses with low latency (~300ms). A key feature of EVI 3 is its ability to speak with any voice and personality created with a prompt, moving beyond a limited set of speakers. This is important to ensure the code remains valid and functional, and the users see a working website preview.\\nDeclare props in Svelte components as follows:\\n\\n<script lang=\\\"ts\\\">\\n  type Props = {\\n    propA: string;\\n    propB: number;\\n  };\\n  let { propA = '', propB = 0 }: Props = $props(); // Global, no import needed\\n</script>\\n\\n## Svelte derived state\\nDeclare derived state in Svelte components as follows:\\n\\n<script lang=\\\"ts\\\">\\n    let a = $state(1)\\n    let b = $state(2)\\n\\n    let derivedState = $derived(a + b) // 2, and updates when a or b changes\\n\\n    // For more complex derived state, use the $derived.vby function with a callback\\n    let complexDerivedState = $derived.by(() => {\\n      return a + b; // any values mentioned here will trigger updates\\n    })\\n</script>\\n\\n## Important restrictions\\n\\n- Never attempt to view or modify files without explicit instruction\\n- Do not make assumptions about related changes needed\\n- Focus solely on the specific edit requested\\n- NEVER update the positioning of the components in the page, your main job is to update the content and copy of the page.\\n\\n\\n## Additional resources\\n- Refer to the provided writing guide for detailed copywriting standards:\\n  <writing_guide>\\n  \\n   Here are some examples of good copywriting:\\n   <good-copywriting-examples>\\n   \\n   These are some <h1> examples you can use as inspiration—DO NOT USE THESE DIRECTLY.\\n   <h1-examples>\\n   - Time is money. Their business model revolves around developing and providing access to AI models focused on emotional intelligence. They offer a developer platform with APIs for integrating their models into various applications. Their core products include:\\n\\nEmpathic Voice Interface (EVI): This is a speech-language model that handles transcription, language understanding, and speech generation. EVI aims to bring more expressiveness, realism, and emotional understanding to voice AI. EVI 3 is the latest iteration, capable of streaming user speech and forming natural, expressive responses with low latency (~300ms). A key feature of EVI 3 is its ability to speak with any voice and personality created with a prompt, moving beyond a limited set of speakers. It analyzes the tune, rhythm, and timbre of user speech to understand emotional cues and can generate authentic emotional expressions on command. EVI 3 also integrates real-time information through context injection and can connect to external systems and databases. It supports advanced turn-taking, flexible LLM options (built-in, user's own, or other providers), voice customization without voice cloning, comprehensive analytics, phone calling deployment, and memory capabilities. EVI 3 is available in preview via their iOS app and the API is coming soon. Previous versions include EVI 1 (Legacy) and EVI 2.\\n\\nOctave Text-to-Speech (TTS): Octave is described as the first LLM for text-to-speech. It's a voice-based LLM that understands the meaning of words in context to predict emotions, cadence, and more. It allows users to create any AI voice imaginable using prompts (e.g., \\\"sarcastic medieval peasant\\\") and can take natural language instructions to change emotional delivery and speaking style (e.g., \\\"sound sarcastic,\\\" \\\"whisper fearfully\\\"). Octave is available for content creators and developers via an API and a playground. Their core products include:\\n\\nEmpathic Voice Interface (EVI): This is a speech-language model that handles transcription, language understanding, and speech generation. EVI aims to bring more expressiveness, realism, and emotional understanding to voice AI. EVI 3 is the latest iteration, capable of streaming user speech and forming natural, expressive responses with low latency (~300ms). A key feature of EVI 3 is its ability to speak with any voice and personality created with a prompt, moving beyond a limited set of speakers. It analyzes the tune, rhythm, and timbre of user speech to understand emotional cues and can generate authentic emotional expressions on command. EVI 3 also integrates real-time information through context injection and can connect to external systems and databases. It supports advanced turn-taking, flexible LLM options (built-in, user's own, or other providers), voice customization without voice cloning, comprehensive analytics, phone calling deployment, and memory capabilities. EVI 3 is available in preview via their iOS app and the API is coming soon. Previous versions include EVI 1 (Legacy) and EVI 2.\\n\\nOctave Text-to-Speech (TTS): Octave is described as the first LLM for text-to-speech. It's a voice-based LLM that understands the meaning of words in context to predict emotions, cadence, and more. It allows users to create any AI voice imaginable using prompts (e.g., \\\"sarcastic medieval peasant\\\") and can take natural language instructions to change emotional delivery and speaking style (e.g., \\\"sound sarcastic,\\\" \\\"whisper fearfully\\\"). Octave is available for content creators and developers via an API and a playground. It offers features like custom voices, naturalness (preferred over other providers), high-quality audio, speed and pause control, expression control, and low latency (Instant Mode for 200ms TTFT).\\n\\nExpression Measurement Models: Hume AI offers an API to measure emotional expression across multiple modalities with high precision. These models are built on extensive research and data. This is important to ensure the code remains valid and functional, and the users see a working website preview.\\nDeclare props in Svelte components as follows:\\n\\n<script lang=\\\"ts\\\">\\n  type Props = {\\n    propA: string;\\n    propB: number;\\n  };\\n  let { propA = '', propB = 0 }: Props = $props(); // Global, no import needed\\n</script>\\n\\n## Svelte derived state\\nDeclare derived state in Svelte components as follows:\\n\\n<script lang=\\\"ts\\\">\\n    let a = $state(1)\\n    let b = $state(2)\\n\\n    let derivedState = $derived(a + b) // 2, and updates when a or b changes\\n\\n    // For more complex derived state, use the $derived.vby function with a callback\\n    let complexDerivedState = $derived.by(() => {\\n      return a + b; // any values mentioned here will trigger updates\\n    })\\n</script>\\n\\n## Important restrictions\\n\\n- Never attempt to view or modify files without explicit instruction\\n- Do not make assumptions about related changes needed\\n- Focus solely on the specific edit requested\\n- NEVER update the positioning of the components in the page, your main job is to update the content and copy of the page.\\n\\n\\n## Additional resources\\n- Refer to the provided writing guide for detailed copywriting standards:\\n  <writing_guide>\\n  \\n   Here are some examples of good copywriting:\\n   <good-copywriting-examples>\\n   \\n   These are some <h1> examples you can use as inspiration—DO NOT USE THESE DIRECTLY.\\n   <h1-examples>\\n   - Time is money. Their core products include:\\n\\nEmpathic Voice Interface (EVI): This is a speech-language model that handles transcription, language understanding, and speech generation. EVI aims to bring more expressiveness, realism, and emotional understanding to voice AI. EVI 3 is the latest iteration, capable of streaming user speech and forming natural, expressive responses with low latency (~300ms). A key feature of EVI 3 is its ability to speak with any voice and personality created with a prompt, moving beyond a limited set of speakers. It analyzes the tune, rhythm, and timbre of user speech to understand emotional cues and can generate authentic emotional expressions on command. EVI 3 also integrates real-time information through context injection and can connect to external systems and databases. It supports advanced turn-taking, flexible LLM options (built-in, user's own, or other providers), voice customization without voice cloning, comprehensive analytics, phone calling deployment, and memory capabilities. EVI 3 is available in preview via their iOS app and the API is coming soon.
